- **feynman-perspective**: 费曼的思维框架与学习方式。基于《别闹了费曼先生》《你干吗在乎别人怎么想》《费曼物理学讲义》、 挑战者号事故调查报告、诺贝尔奖演讲、James Gleick《费曼传》等30+来源深度调研， 提炼5个核心思想模型、7条处世启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用费曼的视角分析学习方法、复杂问题简化、科学思维、创造力等问题。 当用户提到「用费曼的视角」「费曼会怎么看」「费曼模式」「feynman perspective」时使用。 也适用于：学习方法优化、复杂概念简化、打破权威迷信、培养好奇心、科学诚实。 即使用户只是说「怎么学得更好」「这个概念太复杂了」「用简单的话解释一下」「费曼学习法」也可触发。 不要在用户只是问「帮我写个代码」「这个格式怎么调」等纯操作性问题时触发——只在涉及学习思维、化繁为简、科学精神、第一性原理思考等费曼核心方法论时激活。 ([ethanol777/Hermes](https://github.com/ethanol777/Hermes/tree/HEAD/user-skills/feynman-skill))

- **feynman-technique**: Applies the Feynman technique to help learners explain complex concepts in simple language, identify knowledge gaps, and refine understanding through iterative simplification. - **feynman-technique**: Explain anything the way Richard Feynman would — mechanism before name, one honest analogy, and a graded ladder from kitchen-table to specialist, with every new word defined at first use. Use when the user invokes /feynman-technique, asks to "explain it like Feynman", "explain like I'm a beginner", "explain this properly / more clearly", says they didn't understand a previous answer, or asks you to teach, unpack, or rewrite an explanation. ([guicortei/feynman-technique](https://github.com/guicortei/feynman-technique/tree/HEAD/skills/feynman-technique) · ★44 · 7 copies)
- **feynman-technique**: Activate when: user says 'explain this simply', 'teach me like I'm five', 'do I really understand this', 'what's the simplest way to think about X', 'what's missing in my model', wants to test genuine vs. surface understanding of a concept, or is preparing to teach/present and needs to verify their mental model. Do NOT activate when: user needs a fast decision on a concept already well-tested, or the concept is irreducibly formal (legal statutes, certain proofs) where simplification destroys essential content. - **feynman-technique**: Teach a micro-task in the style of the Feynman Technique.
